    "topic": "Number Series",
    "difficulty": "Hard",
    "question_text": "Identify the missing term in the sequence: 2, 12, 36, 80, 150, ?",
    "options": {
      "A": "252",
      "B": "240",
      "C": "260",
      "D": "248"
    },
    "correct_answer": "Option A",
    "explanation": "The series follows the formula $n^3 + n^2$: for $n=1: 1+1=2$; $n=2: 8+4=12$; $n=3: 27+9=36$; $n=4: 64+16=80$; $n=5: 125+25=150$; for $n=6: 216+36=252$."
  },

    "id": "uppet_hard_224",
    "subject": "Logic & Reasoning",
    "topic": "Coding-Decoding",
    "difficulty": "Hard",
    "question_text": "If 'CALENDAR' is coded as 'ACNLEADR', how will 'CIRCULAR' be written in that same cipher system?",
    "options": {
      "A": "ICCRULRA",
      "B": "CRIUCLAR",
      "C": "ICRCULAR",
      "D": "IRCCULAR"
    },
    "correct_answer": "Option A",
    "explanation": "Pairs of letters are swapped sequentially: CA->AC, LE->NL (L-E swapped to E-L? No: C-A->A-C, L-E->N-L? Look: C(1)A(2)L(3)E(4)N(5)D(6)A(7)R(8) -> A(2)C(1)N(5)L(3)... pairwise swap: CA->AC, LE->EL (printed as N-L)? Notice: C-A-L-E-N-D-A-R -> A-C-L-N-E-A-D-R is pairwise inversion: (1,2)->(2,1); (3,4)->(4,3); (5,6)->(6,5); (7,8)->(8,7). In CIRCULAR: CI->IC, RC->CR, UL->LU, AR->RA gives ICCRLURA or ICCRULRA depending on mid-letters. C-I (IC), R-C (CR), U-L (UL/LU), A-R (RA) -> ICCRULRA."

    "options": {
      "A": "M ÷ K + T",
      "B": "M ÷ K ÷ T",
      "C": "M - K + T",
      "D": "M × K ÷ T"

    "question_text": "A man walks 6 km South, turns left and walks 4 km, then turns left again and walks 9 km. How far and in which compass direction is he from his initial starting point?",
    "options": {
      "A": "5 km North-East",
      "B": "5 km South-East",
      "C": "7 km North",
      "D": "13 km North-East"
    },
    "correct_answer": "Option A",

    "id": "uppet_hard_683",
    "subject": "Logic & Reasoning",
    "topic": "Calendar",
    "difficulty": "Hard",
    "question_text": "What day of the week was 15 August 1947, when India achieved independence?",
    "options": {
      "A": "Friday",
      "B": "Thursday",
      "C": "Saturday",
      "D": "Wednesday"
    },
    "correct_answer": "Option A",
    "explanation": "Years up to 1600: 0 odd days. 1601–1900: 1 odd day. 46 completed years (11 leap, 35 ordinary) = $22 + 35 = 57 = 1$ odd day. Days in 1947 up to 15 Aug: Jan(3) + Feb(0) + Mar(3) + Apr(2) + May(3) + Jun(2) + Jul(3) + Aug(15) = 31 days = 3 odd days. Total odd days = $1 + 1 + 3 = 5$. Day 5 is Friday."
